#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

from pathlib import Path
p = Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp')
s = p.read_text()
s = s.replace('#include <chrono>', '#include <chrono>\\n#include <cerrno>\\n#include <climits>')

    if (status "'!= cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s'"\\\\n\", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

size_t parseSize(const char* text, const char* option) {
    char* end = nullptr;
    errno = 0;
    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end "'!= '"'\\\\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
        value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
        fprintf(stderr, \"Invalid value for %s: %s\\\\n\", option, text);
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
    return static_cast<size_t>(value);
}

// Both buffers receive identical fixed boundaries. No boundary copy is needed
// during the iterations, because only interior cells are subsequently written.
__global__ void initializeGrids(Real* first, Real* second, const size_t count) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < count; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
        first[i] = value;
        second[i] = value;
    }
}

// Adjacent lanes traverse adjacent X/Y cells. Each thread advances through a
// short Z column, retaining the previous two plane values in registers.
// Independent Z tiles expose parallelism even for grids with small XY planes.
template <int Depth>
__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
                                 const size_t nx, const size_t plane,
                                 const size_t nz, const size_t planeBlocks) {
    const size_t xy = (blockIdx.x % planeBlocks) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (xy < nx || xy >= plane - nx || xy % nx == 0 || xy % nx == nx - 1) {
        return;
    }
    const size_t firstZ = (blockIdx.x / planeBlocks) * Depth + 1;
    const size_t endZ = firstZ + Depth < nz - 1 ? firstZ + Depth : nz - 1;
    size_t index = firstZ * plane + xy;
    Real bottom = input[index - plane];
    Real center = input[index];
    #pragma unroll
    for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, index += plane) {
        const Real top = input[index + plane];
        const Real sum = center + input[index - 1] + input[index + 1] +
                         input[index - nx] + input[index + nx] + bottom + top;
        // Preserve the original addition order and correctly rounded division.
        output[index] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
        bottom = center;
        center = top;
    }
}

template <int Depth>
void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, const size_t nx,
                   const size_t ny, const size_t nz, cudaStream_t stream) {
    constexpr unsigned int threads = 256;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const size_t planeBlocks = (plane + threads - 1) / threads;
    const size_t zTiles = (nz - 2 + Depth - 1) / Depth;
    stencilIteration<Depth><<<static_cast<unsigned int>(planeBlocks * zTiles), threads, 0, stream>>>(
        input, output, nx, plane, nz, planeBlocks);
}

#include <algorithm>

using Real = double;

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

size_t parseSize(const char* text, const char* option) {
    char* end = nullptr;
    errno = 0;
    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
        value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid value for %s: %s\n", option, text);
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
    return static_cast<size_t>(value);
}

// Both buffers receive identical fixed boundaries. No boundary copy is needed
// during the iterations, because only interior cells are subsequently written.
__global__ void initializeGrids(Real* first, Real* second, const size_t count) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < count; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
        first[i] = value;
        second[i] = value;
    }
}

// Adjacent lanes traverse adjacent X/Y cells. Each thread advances through a
// short Z column, retaining the previous two plane values in registers.
// Independent Z tiles expose parallelism even for grids with small XY planes.
template <int Depth>
__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
                                 const size_t nx, const size_t plane,
                                 const size_t nz, const size_t planeBlocks) {
    const size_t xy = (blockIdx.x % planeBlocks) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (xy < nx || xy >= plane - nx || xy % nx == 0 || xy % nx == nx - 1) {
        return;
    }
    const size_t firstZ = (blockIdx.x / planeBlocks) * Depth + 1;
    const size_t endZ = firstZ + Depth < nz - 1 ? firstZ + Depth : nz - 1;
    size_t index = firstZ * plane + xy;
    Real bottom = input[index - plane];
    Real center = input[index];
    #pragma unroll
    for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, index += plane) {
        const Real top = input[index + plane];
        const Real sum = center + input[index - 1] + input[index + 1] +
                         input[index - nx] + input[index + nx] + bottom + top;
        // Preserve the original addition order and correctly rounded division.
        output[index] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
        bottom = center;
        center = top;
    }
}

template <int Depth>
void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, const size_t nx,
                   const size_t ny, const size_t nz, cudaStream_t stream) {
    constexpr unsigned int threads = 256;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const size_t planeBlocks = (plane + threads - 1) / threads;
    const size_t zTiles = (nz - 2 + Depth - 1) / Depth;
    stencilIteration<Depth><<<static_cast<unsigned int>(planeBlocks * zTiles), threads, 0, stream>>>(
        input, output, nx, plane, nz, planeBlocks);
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = parseSize(argv[++i], "-x");
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = parseSize(argv[++i], "-y");
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = parseSize(argv[++i], "-z");
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            const size_t value = parseSize(argv[++i], "-i");
            if (value > INT_MAX) {
                fprintf(stderr, "Iteration count is too large\n");
                return 1;
            }
            iterations = static_cast<int>(value);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 ||
        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz / sizeof(Real)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Grid dimensions must be positive and fit in memory\n");
        return 1;
    }

    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);
    const bool hasInterior = nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2;

    Real* grid1 = nullptr;
    Real* grid2 = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid1, bytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid2, bytes));

    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    const unsigned int initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
        std::min<size_t>((gridSize + 255) / 256, 65535));
    initializeGrids<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(grid1, grid2, gridSize);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));

    // Small grids need more independent Z tiles to occupy the GPU.
    const bool useColumns = gridSize >= 1024 * 1024;
    auto launch = [&](const Real* input, Real* output) {
        if (useColumns) {
            launchStencil<8>(input, output, nx, ny, nz, stream);
        } else {
            launchStencil<1>(input, output, nx, ny, nz, stream);
        }
    };

    // Replay a bounded batch of iterations to reduce CPU launch overhead. An
    // even-sized batch always starts and finishes in the same buffer.
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
    const int batch = std::min(iterations - iterations % 2, 64);
    if (hasInterior && batch > 0) {
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
        for (int i = 0; i < batch; i += 2) {
            launch(grid1, grid2);
            launch(grid2, grid1);
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
    }

    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    if (hasInterior) {
        int iter = 0;
        if (executable != nullptr) {
            for (; iter + batch <= iterations; iter += batch) {
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(executable, stream));
            }
        }
        for (; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
            launch(iter % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2, iter % 2 == 0 ? grid2 : grid1);
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
    const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    const double seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", seconds * 1000.0);

    const double cellUpdates = hasInterior ?
        static_cast<double>(nx - 2) * (ny - 2) * (nz - 2) * iterations : 0.0;
    const double mcups = seconds > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / seconds / 1e6 : 0.0;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

    std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        finalGrid.resize(gridSize);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(finalGrid.data(), iterations % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2,
                                   bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
    }
    if (executable != nullptr) CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable));
    if (graph != nullptr) CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid2));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid1));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));

    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

        raise SystemExit(1)
    print('Exact match:', (x,y,z,i))

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
a=s.index('template <int Depth>\\n__global__')
b=s.index('bool validateResult',a)
s=s[:a]+'''template <int Depth, typename Index>
__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
                                 const Index nx, const Index plane, const Index nz) {
    const Index xy = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (xy < nx || xy >= plane - nx) return;
    const Index x = xy % nx;
    if (x == 0 || x == nx - 1) return;

    // Grid-striding in Z also supports very long, narrow volumes.
    for (Index firstZ = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.y) * Depth + 1;
         firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += static_cast<Index>(gridDim.y) * Depth) {
        const Index endZ = firstZ + Depth < nz - 1 ? firstZ + Depth : nz - 1;
        Index index = firstZ * plane + xy;
        Real bottom = input[index - plane];
        Real center = input[index];
        #pragma unroll 1
        for (Index z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, index += plane) {
            const Real top = input[index + plane];
            const Real sum = center + input[index - 1] + input[index + 1] +
                             input[index - nx] + input[index + nx] + bottom + top;
            // Preserve the original addition order and correctly rounded division.
            output[index] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
            bottom = center;
            center = top;
        }
    }
}

template <int Depth, typename Index>
void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, const size_t nx,
                   const size_t ny, const size_t nz, cudaStream_t stream) {
    constexpr unsigned int threads = 128;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const size_t planeBlocks = (plane + threads - 1) / threads;
    const size_t zTiles = (nz - 2 + Depth - 1) / Depth;
    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned int>(planeBlocks),
                      static_cast<unsigned int>(std::min<size_t>(zTiles, 65535)));
    stencilIteration<Depth, Index><<<blocks, threads, 0, stream>>>(
        input, output, static_cast<Index>(nx), static_cast<Index>(plane), static_cast<Index>(nz));
}

''' +s[b:]
s=s.replace('''        if (useColumns) {
            launchStencil<8>(input, output, nx, ny, nz, stream);

            launchStencil<1>(input, output, nx, ny, nz, stream);
        }''','''        if (gridSize <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {

            if (useColumns) launchStencil<8, size_t>(input, output, nx, ny, nz, stream);
            else launchStencil<1, size_t>(input, output, nx, ny, nz, stream);
        }''')
s=s.replace('iter + batch <= iterations', 'iter <= iterations - batch')
p.write_text(s)

+
+// Adjacent lanes traverse adjacent X/Y cells. Each thread advances through a
+// short Z column, retaining the previous two plane values in registers.
+// Independent Z tiles expose parallelism even for grids with small XY planes.
+template <int Depth, typename Index>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 const Index nx, const Index plane, const Index nz) {
+    const Index xy = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy < nx || xy >= plane - nx) return;
+    const Index x = xy % nx;
+    if (x == 0 || x == nx - 1) return;
+
+    // Grid-striding in Z also supports very long, narrow volumes.
+    for (Index firstZ = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.y) * Depth + 1;
+         firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += static_cast<Index>(gridDim.y) * Depth) {
+        const Index endZ = firstZ + Depth < nz - 1 ? firstZ + Depth : nz - 1;
+        Index index = firstZ * plane + xy;
+        Real bottom = input[index - plane];
+        Real center = input[index];
+        #pragma unroll 1
+        for (Index z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, index += plane) {
+            const Real top = input[index + plane];
+            const Real sum = center + input[index - 1] + input[index + 1] +
+                             input[index - nx] + input[index + nx] + bottom + top;
+            // Preserve the original addition order and correctly rounded division.
+            output[index] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
+            bottom = center;
+            center = top;
         }
     }
 }
 
+template <int Depth, typename Index>
+void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, const size_t nx,
+                   const size_t ny, const size_t nz, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr unsigned int threads = 128;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t planeBlocks = (plane + threads - 1) / threads;
+    const size_t zTiles = (nz - 2 + Depth - 1) / Depth;
+    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned int>(planeBlocks),
+                      static_cast<unsigned int>(std::min<size_t>(zTiles, 65535)));
+    stencilIteration<Depth, Index><<<blocks, threads, 0, stream>>>(
+        input, output, static_cast<Index>(nx), static_cast<Index>(plane), static_cast<Index>(nz));
+}

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
s=s.replace('''            // Preserve the original addition order and correctly rounded division.
            output[index] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);''', '''            // The reciprocal gives an initial quotient; fused residual and
            // correction steps recover correctly rounded division by seven.
            // All stencil sums are finite, nonnegative, and bounded by 126.
            constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
            const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
            const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
            output[index] = __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);''')
p.write_text(s)

import ctypes as C
nvrtc=C.CDLL('libnvrtc.so.12')
cu=C.CDLL('libcuda.so.1')
source=b'''
extern \"C\" __global__ void verify(unsigned int* mismatches) {
    unsigned long long seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
    for (int i=0; i<64; ++i) {
        seed = seed * 6364136223846793005ULL + 1442695040888963407ULL;
        const unsigned long long bits = ((1010ULL + (seed >> 52) % 20) << 52) |
                                        (seed & ((1ULL << 52) - 1));
        double sum = __longlong_as_double(bits);
        if (sum > 126.0) sum = 126.0;
        if (i==0) sum = (seed % 127);
        const double r = 1.0/7.0;
        const double q = __dmul_rn(sum,r);
        const double residual = __fma_rn(-7.0,q,sum);
        const double result = __fma_rn(residual,r,q);
        const double reference = __ddiv_rn(sum,7.0);
        if (__double_as_longlong(result) "'!= __double_as_longlong(reference)) {
            atomicAdd(mismatches,1U);
        }
    }
}
'"'''
def check(code):
    assert code==0,code

/bin/bash -lc "python3 -u - <<'PY'
import ctypes as C
from pathlib import Path
from statistics import median
nvrtc=C.CDLL('libnvrtc.so.12')
cu=C.CDLL('libcuda.so.1')
s=Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp').read_text()
a=s.index('template <int Depth, typename Index>\\n__global__')
b=s.index('template <int Depth, typename Index>\\nvoid launchStencil',a)
source=('using Real=double;\\n'+s[a:b]+'''
extern \"C\" __global__ void init(double* a, double* b, unsigned long long n) {
    for (unsigned long long i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x; i<n; i+=(unsigned long long)gridDim.x*blockDim.x) a[i]=b[i]=double(i%19);
}
''').encode()
def check(code):
    assert code==0,code
program=C.c_void_p()
check(nvrtc.nvrtcCreateProgram(C.byref(program),source,b'tuning.cu',0,None,None))
depths=[1,2,4,8,16,32]
names={d:f'&stencilIteration<{d}, unsigned int>'.encode() for d in depths}
for name in names.values(): check(nvrtc.nvrtcAddNameExpression(program,name))
options=(C.c_char_p*2)(b'--gpu-architecture=compute_86',b'--fmad=false')
code=nvrtc.nvrtcCompileProgram(program,2,options)
logsize=C.c_size_t()
nvrtc.nvrtcGetProgramLogSize(program,C.byref(logsize))
log=C.create_string_buffer(logsize.value)
nvrtc.nvrtcGetProgramLog(program,log)
if code: print(log.value.decode())
check(code)
size=C.c_size_t()
check(nvrtc.nvrtcGetPTXSize(program,C.byref(size)))
ptx=C.create_string_buffer(size.value)
check(nvrtc.nvrtcGetPTX(program,ptx))
check(cu.cuInit(0))
context=C.c_void_p()
check(cu.cuDevicePrimaryCtxRetain(C.byref(context),0))
check(cu.cuCtxSetCurrent(context))
module=C.c_void_p()
check(cu.cuModuleLoadData(C.byref(module),ptx))
functions={}
for d,name in names.items():
    lowered=C.c_char_p()
    check(nvrtc.nvrtcGetLoweredName(program,name,C.byref(lowered)))
    f=C.c_void_p()
    check(cu.cuModuleGetFunction(C.byref(f),module,lowered))
    functions[d]=f
initialize=C.c_void_p()
check(cu.cuModuleGetFunction(C.byref(initialize),module,b'init'))
first=C.c_uint64(); second=C.c_uint64()
N=512**3
for p in (first,second): check(cu.cuMemAlloc_v2(C.byref(p),C.c_size_t(N*8)))
count=C.c_uint64(N)
args=(C.c_void_p*3)(C.addressof(first),C.addressof(second),C.addressof(count))
check(cu.cuLaunchKernel(initialize,65535,1,1,256,1,1,0,None,args,None))
check(cu.cuCtxSynchronize())
start=C.c_void_p(); end=C.c_void_p()
check(cu.cuEventCreate(C.byref(start),0));check(cu.cuEventCreate(C.byref(end),0))
for side,iters in [(128,100),(256,40),(512,20)]:
    nx=C.c_uint(side);plane=C.c_uint(side*side);nz=C.c_uint(side)
    params=(C.c_void_p*5)(C.addressof(first),C.addressof(second),C.addressof(nx),C.addressof(plane),C.addressof(nz))
    reverse=(C.c_void_p*5)(C.addressof(second),C.addressof(first),C.addressof(nx),C.addressof(plane),C.addressof(nz))
    results=[]
    for d in depths:
        for threads in [64,128,256]:
            blocks=(side*side+threads-1)//threads
            zs=(side-2+d-1)//d
            f=functions[d]
            def launch(i):
                check(cu.cuLaunchKernel(f,blocks,zs,1,threads,1,1,0,None,params if i%2==0 else reverse,None))
            for i in range(4): launch(i)
            timings=[]
            for repeat in range(2):
                check(cu.cuEventRecord(start,None))
                for i in range(iters): launch(i)
                check(cu.cuEventRecord(end,None));check(cu.cuEventSynchronize(end))
                ms=C.c_float()
                check(cu.cuEventElapsedTime(C.byref(ms),start,end))
                timings.append(ms.value)
            results.append((median(timings),d,threads))
    print('Grid',side,'iterations',iters,flush=True)
    for ms,d,t in sorted(results): print(f'  depth={d:2} threads={t:3}: {ms:8.3f} ms',flush=True)
for event in (start,end): check(cu.cuEventDestroy_v2(event))
for p in (first,second):check(cu.cuMemFree_v2(p))
check(cu.cuModuleUnload(module));check(cu.cuDevicePrimaryCtxRelease(0))
check(nvrtc.nvrtcDestroyProgram(C.byref(program)))

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
a=s.index('// Adjacent lanes traverse')
s=s[:a]+'''// Since seven is an odd integer, a normal binary quotient cannot be halfway
// between doubles. The exact fused residual and one correction therefore give
// the same rounding as division for these sums (zero, or normal values <= 126).
__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(const Real sum) {
    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
}

// For narrow XY planes, distribute threads across Z as well so that a warp
// contains many interior points rather than mostly boundary lanes.
template <typename Index>
__global__ void stencilFlat(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
                            const Index nx, const Index plane, const Index count) {
    for (size_t position = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         position < count; position += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
        const Index index = static_cast<Index>(position);
        if (index < plane || index >= count - plane) continue;
        const Index xy = index % plane;
        if (xy < nx || xy >= plane - nx) continue;
        const Index x = xy % nx;
        if (x == 0 || x == nx - 1) continue;
        const Real sum = input[index] + input[index - 1] + input[index + 1] +
                         input[index - nx] + input[index + nx] +
                         input[index - plane] + input[index + plane];
        output[index] = averageSeven(sum);
    }
}

''' +s[a:]
s=s.replace('''            // The reciprocal gives an initial quotient; fused residual and
            // correction steps recover correctly rounded division by seven.
            // All stencil sums are finite, nonnegative, and bounded by 126.
            constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
            const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
            const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
            output[index] = __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);''','''            output[index] = averageSeven(sum);''')
s=s.replace('''    constexpr unsigned int threads = 128;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const size_t planeBlocks''','''    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    if (plane < 128) {
        const size_t count = plane * nz;
        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
            std::min<size_t>((count + 255) / 256, 65535));
        stencilFlat<Index><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(input, output,
            static_cast<Index>(nx), static_cast<Index>(plane), static_cast<Index>(count));
        return;
    }
    const unsigned int threads = plane >= 65536 ? 256 : 128;
    const size_t planeBlocks''')
s=s.replace('''    auto launch = [&](const Real* input, Real* output) {''','''    // Use cheaper 32-bit index arithmetic when the entire allocation fits;
    // retain a 64-bit kernel for GPUs capable of holding larger grids.
    auto launch = [&](const Real* input, Real* output) {''')
p.write_text(s)

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cerrno>
#include <climits>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

size_t parseSize(const char* text, const char* option) {
    char* end = nullptr;
    errno = 0;
    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
        value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid value for %s: %s\n", option, text);
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
    return static_cast<size_t>(value);
}

// Both buffers receive identical fixed boundaries. No boundary copy is needed
// during the iterations, because only interior cells are subsequently written.
__global__ void initializeGrids(Real* first, Real* second, const size_t count) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < count; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
        first[i] = value;
        second[i] = value;
    }
}

// Since seven is an odd integer, a normal binary quotient cannot be halfway
// between doubles. The exact fused residual and one correction therefore give
// the same rounding as division for these sums (zero, or normal values <= 126).
__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(const Real sum) {
    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
}

// For narrow XY planes, distribute threads across Z as well so that a warp
// contains many interior points rather than mostly boundary lanes.
template <typename Index>
__global__ void stencilFlat(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
                            const Index nx, const Index plane, const Index count) {
    for (size_t position = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         position < count; position += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
        const Index index = static_cast<Index>(position);
        if (index < plane || index >= count - plane) continue;
        const Index xy = index % plane;
        if (xy < nx || xy >= plane - nx) continue;
        const Index x = xy % nx;
        if (x == 0 || x == nx - 1) continue;
        const Real sum = input[index] + input[index - 1] + input[index + 1] +
                         input[index - nx] + input[index + nx] +
                         input[index - plane] + input[index + plane];
        output[index] = averageSeven(sum);
    }
}

// Adjacent lanes traverse adjacent X/Y cells. Each thread advances through a
// short Z column, retaining the previous two plane values in registers.
// Independent Z tiles expose parallelism even for grids with small XY planes.
template <int Depth, typename Index>
__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
                                 const Index nx, const Index plane, const Index nz) {
    const Index xy = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (xy < nx || xy >= plane - nx) return;
    const Index x = xy % nx;
    if (x == 0 || x == nx - 1) return;

    // Grid-striding in Z also supports very long, narrow volumes.
    for (Index firstZ = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.y) * Depth + 1;
         firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += static_cast<Index>(gridDim.y) * Depth) {
        const Index endZ = firstZ + Depth < nz - 1 ? firstZ + Depth : nz - 1;
        Index index = firstZ * plane + xy;
        Real bottom = input[index - plane];
        Real center = input[index];
        #pragma unroll 1
        for (Index z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, index += plane) {
            const Real top = input[index + plane];
            const Real sum = center + input[index - 1] + input[index + 1] +
                             input[index - nx] + input[index + nx] + bottom + top;
            output[index] = averageSeven(sum);
            bottom = center;
            center = top;
        }
    }
}

template <int Depth, typename Index>
void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, const size_t nx,
                   const size_t ny, const size_t nz, cudaStream_t stream) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    if (plane < 128) {
        const size_t count = plane * nz;
        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
            std::min<size_t>((count + 255) / 256, 65535));
        stencilFlat<Index><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(input, output,
            static_cast<Index>(nx), static_cast<Index>(plane), static_cast<Index>(count));
        return;
    }
    const unsigned int threads = plane >= 65536 ? 256 : 128;
    const size_t planeBlocks = (plane + threads - 1) / threads;
    const size_t zTiles = (nz - 2 + Depth - 1) / Depth;
    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned int>(planeBlocks),
                      static_cast<unsigned int>(std::min<size_t>(zTiles, 65535)));
    stencilIteration<Depth, Index><<<blocks, threads, 0, stream>>>(
        input, output, static_cast<Index>(nx), static_cast<Index>(plane), static_cast<Index>(nz));
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

-project(stencil3d_benchmark)
+cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
+
+# Optimize for the installed GPU unless the caller selects target architectures.
+if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
+    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
+endif()
+project(stencil3d_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)
 
 set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
 set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
+set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 20)
+set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
 
+set_source_files_properties(stencil3d.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
 add_executable(stencil3d stencil3d.cpp)
 
 # Enable optimizations
-target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)
+target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE
+    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;--fmad=false;-Xcompiler=-O3,-march=native,-Wall,-Wextra>
+)

 target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)
